     1→import Veil
     2→
     3→-- source:https://github.com/DistAlgo/proofs/blob/master/basic-paxos/PaxosLam.tla
     4→-- ------------------------------- MODULE Paxos -------------------------------
     5→-- (***************************************************************************)
     6→-- (* This is a TLA+ specification of the Paxos Consensus algorithm,          *)
     7→-- (* described in                                                            *)
     8→-- (*                                                                         *)
     9→-- (*  Paxos Made Simple:                                                     *)
    10→-- (*   http://research.microsoft.com/en-us/um/people/lamport/pubs/pubs.html#paxos-simple *)
    11→-- (*                                                                         *)
    12→-- (* and a TLAPS-checked proof of its correctness.  This was mostly done as  *)
    13→-- (* a test to see how the SMT backend of TLAPS is now working.              *)
    14→-- (***************************************************************************)
    15→-- EXTENDS Integers, TLAPS, TLC
    16→
    17→veil module Paxos
    18→
    19→-- CONSTANTS Acceptors, Values, Quorums
    20→type acceptor
    21→type value
    22→type quorum
    23→type ballot
    24→
    25→-- ASSUME QuorumAssumption ==
    26→--           /\ Quorums \subseteq SUBSET Acceptors
    27→--           /\ \A Q1, Q2 \in Quorums : Q1 \cap Q2 # {}
    28→
    29→-- (***************************************************************************)
    30→-- (* The following lemma is an immediate consequence of the assumption.      *)
    31→-- (***************************************************************************)
    32→-- LEMMA QuorumNonEmpty == \A Q \in Quorums : Q # {}
    33→-- BY QuorumAssumption
    34→
    35→-- Ballots == Nat
    36→
    37→instantiate tot : TotalOrderWithZero ballot
    38→open TotalOrderWithZero
    39→immutable individual validBallots : List ballot
    40→immutable relation member (A : acceptor) (Q : quorum)
    41→
    42→-- VARIABLES msgs,    \* The set of messages that have been sent.
    43→--           maxBal,  \* maxBal[a] is the highest-number ballot acceptor a
    44→--                    \*   has participated in.
    45→--           maxVBal, \* maxVBal[a] is the highest ballot in which a has
    46→--           maxVal   \*   voted, and maxVal[a] is the value it voted for
    47→--                    \*   in that ballot.
    48→
    49→-- vars == <<msgs, maxBal, maxVBal, maxVal>>
    50→
    51→
    52→-- None == CHOOSE v : v \notin Values
    53→
    54→-- LEMMA NoneNotAValue == None \notin Values
    55→-- BY NoSetContainsEverything DEF None
    56→
    57→type MsgSet
    58→type AcceptorSet
    59→
    60→-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→-- (***************************************************************************)
    62→-- (* This section of the spec defines the invariant Inv.                     *)
    63→-- (***************************************************************************)
    64→-- Messages ==      [type : {"1a"}, bal : Ballots]
    65→--             \cup [type : {"1b"}, bal : Ballots, maxVBal : Ballots \cup {-1},
    66→--                     maxVal : Values \cup {None}, acc : Acceptors]
    67→--             \cup [type : {"2a"}, bal : Ballots, val : Values]
    68→--             \cup [type : {"2b"}, bal : Ballots, val : Values, acc : Acceptors]
    69→
    70→@[veil_decl]
    71→inductive MsgType where
    72→  | Phase1a
    73→  | Phase1b
    74→  | Phase2a
    75→  | Phase2b
    76→deriving instance Veil.Enumeration for MsgType
    77→
    78→@[veil_decl]
    79→structure Msg (ac val blt : Type) where
    80→  msgType : MsgType
    81→  acc : ac
    82→  val : val
    83→  bal : blt
    84→  maxVBal : blt
    85→deriving instance Veil.Enumeration for Msg
    86→
    87→instantiate msgTset : TSet (Msg acceptor value ballot) MsgSet
    88→instantiate acSet : TSet acceptor AcceptorSet
    89→
    90→individual msgs : MsgSet
    91→function maxVBal (a : acceptor) : ballot
    92→function maxBal (a : acceptor) : ballot
    93→function maxVal (a : acceptor) : value
    94→immutable individual AcceptorsUNIV : List acceptor
    95→
    96→#gen_state
    97→
    98→-- Init == /\ msgs = {}
    99→--         /\ maxVBal = [a \in Acceptors |-> -1]
   100→--         /\ maxBal  = [a \in Acceptors |-> -1]
   101→--         /\ maxVal  = [a \in Acceptors |-> None]
   102→
   103→theory ghost relation lt (x y : ballot) := (tot.le x y ∧ x ≠ y)
   104→theory ghost relation next (x y : ballot) := (lt x y ∧ ∀ z, lt x z → tot.le y z)
   105→theory ghost relation ge (x y : ballot) := (tot.le y x)
   106→theory ghost relation gt (x y : ballot) := (tot.le y x ∧ x ≠ y)
   107→
   108→assumption [quorum_intersection]
   109→  ∀ (q1 q2 : quorum), ∃ (r : acceptor), member r q1 ∧ member r q2
   110→
   111→assumption [AcceptorsUNIV_complete] ∀ (a : acceptor), a ∈ AcceptorsUNIV
   112→
   113→after_init {
   114→  msgs := msgTset.empty
   115→  maxVBal A := tot.zero
   116→  maxBal A := tot.zero
   117→  maxVal A := (default : value)
   118→}
   119→
   120→-- Send(m) == msgs' = msgs \cup {m}
   121→procedure Send (m : Msg acceptor value ballot) {
   122→  msgs := msgTset.insert m msgs
   123→}
   124→
   125→-- (***************************************************************************)
   126→-- (* Phase 1a: A leader selects a ballot number b and sends a 1a message     *)
   127→-- (* with ballot b to a majority of acceptors.  It can do this only if it    *)
   128→-- (* has not already sent a 1a message for ballot b.                         *)
   129→-- (***************************************************************************)
   130→-- Phase1a(b) == /\ ~ \E m \in msgs : (m.type = "1a") /\ (m.bal = b)
   131→--               /\ Send([type |-> "1a", bal |-> b])
   132→--               /\ UNCHANGED <<maxVBal, maxBal, maxVal>>
   133→action Phase1a (b : ballot){
   134→  require b ≠ zero
   135→  let filterMsgs := msgTset.filter msgs (fun m => m.msgType == MsgType.Phase1a && m.bal == b)
   136→  require msgTset.count filterMsgs = 0
   137→  let sentMsg : Msg acceptor value ballot := {
   138→    msgType := MsgType.Phase1a,
   139→    bal := b,
   140→    /-Unused variable-/
   141→    acc := default,
   142→    val := default,
   143→    maxVBal := default
   144→  }
   145→  Send sentMsg
   146→}
   147→
   148→-- (***************************************************************************)
   149→-- (* Phase 1b: If an acceptor receives a 1a message with ballot b greater    *)
   150→-- (* than that of any 1a message to which it has already responded, then it  *)
   151→-- (* responds to the request with a promise not to accept any more proposals *)
   152→-- (* for ballots numbered less than b and with the highest-numbered ballot   *)
   153→-- (* (if any) for which it has voted for a value and the value it voted for  *)
   154→-- (* in that ballot.  That promise is made in a 1b message.                  *)
   155→-- (***************************************************************************)
   156→-- Phase1b(a) ==
   157→--   \E m \in msgs :
   158→--      /\ m.type = "1a"
   159→--      /\ m.bal > maxBal[a]
   160→--      /\ Send([type |-> "1b", bal |-> m.bal, maxVBal |-> maxVBal[a],
   161→--                maxVal |-> maxVal[a], acc |-> a])
   162→--      /\ maxBal' = [maxBal EXCEPT ![a] = m.bal]
   163→--      /\ UNCHANGED <<maxVBal, maxVal>>
   164→
   165→action Phase1b (a : acceptor) {
   166→  -- let m :| msgTset.contains m msgs ∧ m.msgType = MsgType.Phase1a
   167→  let filteredMsgs := msgTset.filter msgs (fun m =>
   168→    m.msgType == MsgType.Phase1a && decide (gt m.bal (maxBal a)))
   169→  let m :| msgTset.contains m filteredMsgs
   170→  -- require gt m.bal (maxBal a)
   171→  let replyMsg : Msg acceptor value ballot := {
   172→    msgType := MsgType.Phase1b,
   173→    acc := a,
   174→    val := maxVal a,
   175→    bal := m.bal,
   176→    maxVBal := maxVBal a
   177→  }
   178→  Send replyMsg
   179→  maxBal a := m.bal
   180→}
   181→
   182→-- (***************************************************************************)
   183→-- (* Phase 2a: If the leader receives a response to its 1b message (for      *)
   184→-- (* ballot b) from a quorum of acceptors, then it sends a 2a message to all *)
   185→-- (* acceptors for a proposal in ballot b with a value v, where v is the     *)
   186→-- (* value of the highest-numbered proposal among the responses, or is any   *)
   187→-- (* value if the responses reported no proposals.  The leader can send only *)
   188→-- (* one 2a message for any ballot.                                          *)
   189→-- (***************************************************************************)
   190→-- Phase2a(b) ==
   191→--   /\ ~ \E m \in msgs : (m.type = "2a") /\ (m.bal = b)
   192→--   /\ \E v \in Values :
   193→--        /\ \E Q \in Quorums :
   194→--             \E S \in SUBSET {m \in msgs : (m.type = "1b") /\ (m.bal = b)} :
   195→--                /\ \A a \in Q : \E m \in S : m.acc = a
   196→--                /\ \/ \A m \in S : m.maxVBal = -1
   197→--                   \/ \E c \in 0..(b-1) :
   198→--                         /\ \A m \in S : m.maxVBal =< c
   199→--                         /\ \E m \in S : /\ m.maxVBal = c
   200→--                                         /\ m.maxVal = v
   201→--        /\ Send([type |-> "2a", bal |-> b, val |-> v])
   202→--   /\ UNCHANGED <<maxBal, maxVBal, maxVal>>
   203→
   204→-- Imperative version: Q is an action parameter, S is picked non-deterministically
   205→-- from SUBSET of 1b messages, and v is computed from S
   206→-- Optimization: instead of picking MsgSet (huge), pick AcceptorSet (only 2^n possibilities)
   207→action Phase2a (b : ballot) {
   208→  require b ≠ zero
   209→  let filterMsgs := msgTset.filter msgs (fun m =>
   210→    decide $ m.msgType = MsgType.Phase2a ∧ m.bal = b)
   211→  require msgTset.count filterMsgs = 0
   212→  let v ← pick value
   213→  let Q ← pick quorum
   214→  let all1bMsgs := msgTset.filter msgs (fun m =>
   215→    decide $ m.msgType = MsgType.Phase1b ∧ m.bal = b)
   216→
   217→  /- Instead of picking S from the set of all subsets of messages,
   218→    we pick a subset of acceptors, and construct S by filtering messages from those
   219→    acceptors. As we only care about condition `m.acc = a`.
   220→    This reduces the non-deterministic choices to 2^|acceptors|.  -/
   221→  let selectedAcceptors ← pick AcceptorSet
   222→  -- forbid picking acceptors that don't have a 1b for ballot b
   223→  require (acSet.toList selectedAcceptors).all (fun a =>
   224→    (msgTset.toList all1bMsgs).any (fun m => decide (m.acc = a)))
   225→  let S := msgTset.filter all1bMsgs (fun m => acSet.contains m.acc selectedAcceptors)
   226→
   227→  let quorumCovered := AcceptorsUNIV |>.all (fun a =>
   228→    /- `/\ \A a \in Q : \E m \in S : m.acc = a, member a Q → (∃m ∈ S, m.acc = a)`-/
   229→    !member a Q || (msgTset.toList S |>.any (fun m => decide (m.acc = a))))
   230→  require quorumCovered
   231→  -- \/ \A m \in S : m.maxVBal = -1
   232→  -- \/ \E c \in 0..(b-1) : /\ \A m \in S : m.maxVBal =< c
   233→  --                        /\ \E m \in S : m.maxVBal = c /\ m.maxVal = v
   234→  let sList := msgTset.toList S
   235→  let allzero := sList.all (fun m => decide (m.maxVBal = zero))
   236→  let vb := validBallots.any (fun c =>
   237→    (decide $ lt c b) ∧
   238→    sList.all (fun m => decide (tot.le m.maxVBal c)) ∧
   239→    sList.any (fun m => decide (m.maxVBal = c ∧ m.val = v)))
   240→  require allzero ∨ vb
   241→  let sentMsg : Msg acceptor value ballot := {
   242→    msgType := MsgType.Phase2a,
   243→    val := v,
   244→    bal := b,
   245→    acc := default,
   246→    maxVBal := default
   247→    }
   248→  Send sentMsg
   249→}
   250→-- (***************************************************************************)
   251→-- (* Phase 2b: If an acceptor receives a 2a message for a ballot numbered    *)
   252→-- (* b, it votes for the message's value in ballot b unless it has already   *)
   253→-- (* responded to a 1a request for a ballot number greater than or equal to  *)
   254→-- (* b.                                                                      *)
   255→-- (***************************************************************************)
   256→-- Phase2b(a) ==
   257→--   \E m \in msgs :
   258→--     /\ m.type = "2a"
   259→--     /\ m.bal >= maxBal[a]
   260→--     /\ Send([type |-> "2b", bal |-> m.bal, val |-> m.val, acc |-> a])
   261→--     /\ maxVBal' = [maxVBal EXCEPT ![a] = m.bal]
   262→--     /\ maxBal' = [maxBal EXCEPT ![a] = m.bal]
   263→--     /\ maxVal' = [maxVal EXCEPT ![a] = m.val]
   264→
   265→action Phase2b (a : acceptor) {
   266→  -- let m :| msgTset.contains m msgs ∧ m.msgType = MsgType.Phase2a
   267→  -- require ge m.bal (maxBal a)
   268→  let filteredMsgs := msgTset.filter msgs (fun m =>
   269→    decide $ m.msgType = MsgType.Phase2a ∧ ge m.bal (maxBal a))
   270→  let m :| msgTset.contains m filteredMsgs
   271→  let replyMsg : Msg acceptor value ballot := {
   272→    msgType := MsgType.Phase2b,
   273→    acc := a,
   274→    val := m.val,
   275→    bal := m.bal,
   276→    maxVBal := default
   277→  }
   278→  Send replyMsg
   279→  maxVBal a := m.bal
   280→  maxBal a := m.bal
   281→  maxVal a := m.val
   282→}
   283→
   284→-- Next == \/ \E b \in Ballots : Phase1a(b) \/ Phase2a(b)
   285→--         \/ \E a \in Acceptors : Phase1b(a) \/ Phase2b(a)
   286→
   287→-- Spec == Init /\ [][Next]_vars
   288→-- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   289→-- (***************************************************************************)
   290→-- (* How a value is chosen:                                                  *)
   291→-- (*                                                                         *)
   292→-- (* This spec does not contain any actions in which a value is explicitly   *)
   293→-- (* chosen (or a chosen value learned).  Wnat it means for a value to be    *)
   294→-- (* chosen is defined by the operator Chosen, where Chosen(v) means that v  *)
   295→-- (* has been chosen.  From this definition, it is obvious how a process     *)
   296→-- (* learns that a value has been chosen from messages of type "2b".         *)
   297→-- (***************************************************************************)
   298→-- VotedForIn(a, v, b) == \E m \in msgs : /\ m.type = "2b"
   299→--                                        /\ m.val  = v
   300→--                                        /\ m.bal  = b
   301→--                                        /\ m.acc  = a
   302→
   303→-- ChosenIn(v, b) == \E Q \in Quorums :
   304→--                      \A a \in Q : VotedForIn(a, v, b)
   305→
   306→-- Chosen(v) == \E b \in Ballots : ChosenIn(v, b)
   307→ghost relation VotedForIn (a : acceptor) (v : value) (b : ballot) :=
   308→  ∃ m, msgTset.contains m msgs ∧ m.msgType = MsgType.Phase2b ∧ m.val = v ∧ m.bal = b ∧ m.acc = a
   309→ghost relation ChosenIn (v : value) (b : ballot) :=
   310→  ∃ Q, ∀ a, member a Q → VotedForIn a v b
   311→ghost relation Chosen (v : value) :=
   312→  ∃ b, ChosenIn v b
   313→
   314→
   315→-- (***************************************************************************)
   316→-- (* The consistency condition that a consensus algorithm must satisfy is    *)
   317→-- (* the invariance of the following state predicate Consistency.            *)
   318→-- (***************************************************************************)
   319→-- Consistency == \A v1, v2 \in Values : Chosen(v1) /\ Chosen(v2) => (v1 = v2)
   320→safety [Consistency] ∀ v1 v2, Chosen v1 → Chosen v2 → v1 = v2
   321→
   322→
   323→-- TypeOK == /\ msgs \in SUBSET Messages
   324→--           /\ maxVBal \in [Acceptors -> Ballots \cup {-1}]
   325→--           /\ maxBal \in  [Acceptors -> Ballots \cup {-1}]
   326→--           /\ maxVal \in  [Acceptors -> Values \cup {None}]
   327→--           /\ \A a \in Acceptors : maxBal[a] >= maxVBal[a]
   328→
   329→-- (***************************************************************************)
   330→-- (* WontVoteIn(a, b) is a predicate that implies that a has not voted and   *)
   331→-- (* never will vote in ballot b.                                            *)
   332→-- (***************************************************************************)
   333→-- WontVoteIn(a, b) == /\ \A v \in Values : ~ VotedForIn(a, v, b)
   334→--                     /\ maxBal[a] > b
   335→ghost relation WontVoteIn (a : acceptor) (b : ballot) :=
   336→  (∀ v, ¬ VotedForIn a v b) ∧ gt (maxBal a) b
   337→-- (***************************************************************************)
   338→-- (* The predicate SafeAt(v, b) implies that no value other than perhaps v   *)
   339→-- (* has been or ever will be chosen in any ballot numbered less than b.     *)
   340→-- (***************************************************************************)
   341→-- SafeAt(v, b) ==
   342→--   \A c \in 0..(b-1) :
   343→--     \E Q \in Quorums :
   344→--       \A a \in Q : VotedForIn(a, v, c) \/ WontVoteIn(a, c)
   345→ghost relation SafeAt (v : value) (b : ballot) :=
   346→  ∀ c, lt c b →
   347→    ∃ Q, ∀ a, member a Q → (VotedForIn a v c ∨ WontVoteIn a c)
   348→
   349→
   350→-- TypeOK: maxBal[a] >= maxVBal[a] for all acceptors
   351→-- (other conjuncts are type-level in Lean)
   352→invariant [TypeOK] ∀ a, ge (maxBal a) (maxVBal a)
   353→
   354→-- MsgInv for 1b messages
   355→invariant [MsgInv1b] ∀ m, msgTset.contains m msgs → m.msgType = MsgType.Phase1b →
   356→  -- m.bal <= maxBal[m.acc]
   357→  tot.le m.bal (maxBal m.acc) ∧
   358→  -- Either (maxVBal in Ballots ∧ VotedForIn) or (maxVBal = -1)
   359→  ((m.maxVBal ≠ zero ∧ VotedForIn m.acc m.val m.maxVBal) ∨ (m.maxVBal = zero)) ∧
   360→  -- For all c in (maxVBal+1)..(bal-1), no vote exists
   361→  (∀ c, lt m.maxVBal c → lt c m.bal → ∀ v, ¬VotedForIn m.acc v c)
   362→
   363→-- MsgInv for 2a messages
   364→invariant [MsgInv2a] ∀ m, msgTset.contains m msgs → m.msgType = MsgType.Phase2a →
   365→  -- SafeAt(m.val, m.bal)
   366→  SafeAt m.val m.bal ∧
   367→  -- Unique 2a message per ballot
   368→  (∀ ma, msgTset.contains ma msgs → ma.msgType = MsgType.Phase2a → ma.bal = m.bal → ma = m)
   369→
   370→-- MsgInv for 2b messages
   371→invariant [MsgInv2b] ∀ m, msgTset.contains m msgs → m.msgType = MsgType.Phase2b →
   372→  -- There exists a 2a message with same ballot and value
   373→  (∃ ma, msgTset.contains ma msgs ∧ ma.msgType = MsgType.Phase2a ∧ ma.bal = m.bal ∧ ma.val = m.val) ∧
   374→  -- m.bal <= maxVBal[m.acc]
   375→  tot.le m.bal (maxVBal m.acc)
   376→
   377→-- AccInv: acceptor invariants
   378→invariant [AccInv] ∀ a,
   379→  -- maxVBal[a] <= maxBal[a]
   380→  tot.le (maxVBal a) (maxBal a) ∧
   381→  -- (maxVBal[a] >= 0) => VotedForIn(a, maxVal[a], maxVBal[a])
   382→  (maxVBal a ≠ zero → VotedForIn a (maxVal a) (maxVBal a)) ∧
   383→  -- For all c > maxVBal[a], no vote exists
   384→  (∀ c, gt c (maxVBal a) → ∀ v, ¬VotedForIn a v c)
   385→
   386→#gen_spec
   387→
   388→
   389→theorem Phase1a_doesNotThrow (ρ : Type) (σ : Type) (acceptor : Type) [acceptor_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} acceptor]
   390→    [acceptor_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} acceptor] (value : Type) [value_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} value]
   391→    [value_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} value] (quorum : Type) [quorum_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} quorum]
   392→    [quorum_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} quorum] (ballot : Type) [ballot_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} ballot]
   393→    [ballot_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} ballot] [tot : TotalOrderWithZero ballot] (MsgSet : Type)
   394→    [MsgSet_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} MsgSet] [MsgSet_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} MsgSet] (AcceptorSet : Type)
   395→    [AcceptorSet_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} AcceptorSet] [AcceptorSet_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} AcceptorSet]
   396→    [msgTset : TSet (Msg acceptor value ballot) MsgSet] [acSet : TSet acceptor AcceptorSet] (χ : State.Label → Type)
   397→    [χ_rep :

   472→\*          BY <2>1, <4>1a, <4>3, <4>4, <4>6, <4>11, SafeAtStable \*, Zenon
   473→\*            DEF MsgInv, TypeOK, Messages
   474→
   475→\*        <5>3. m.type = "2b"
   476→\*               => (/\ \E ma \in msgs :
   477→\*                         /\ ma.type = "2a"
   478→\*                         /\ ma.bal = m.bal
   479→\*                         /\ ma.val = m.val
   480→\*                   /\ m.bal =< maxVBal[m.acc])'
   481→\*          BY <2>1, <4>1a, <4>3, <4>4, <4>6, <4>11, SafeAtStable\* , Zenon
   482→\*            DEF MsgInv, TypeOK, Messages
   483→
   484→\*        <5>4. QED
   485→\*          BY <5>1, <5>2, <5>3 
   486→\* *)
   487→\*     <3>4. ASSUME NEW a \in Acceptors, Phase2b(a)
   488→\*           PROVE  MsgInv'
   489→\*       <4>. PICK m \in msgs : Phase2b(a)!(m)
   490→\*         BY <3>4 DEF Phase2b
   491→\*       <4>1. \A aa, vv, bb : VotedForIn(aa,vv,bb) => VotedForIn(aa,vv,bb)'
   492→\*         BY DEF VotedForIn, Send
   493→\*       <4>2. \A mm \in msgs : mm.type = "1b"
   494→\*                => \A v \in Values, c \in (mm.maxVBal+1) .. (mm.bal-1) :
   495→\*                      ~ VotedForIn(mm.acc, v, c) => ~ VotedForIn(mm.acc, v, c)'
   496→\*         BY DEF Send, VotedForIn, MsgInv, TypeOK, Messages
   497→\*       <4>. QED
   498→\*         BY <4>1, <4>2, SafeAtStable, <2>1 DEF MsgInv, Send, TypeOK, Messages
   499→\*     <3>5. QED
   500→\*       BY <3>1, <3>2, <3>3, <3>4 DEF Next
   501→\*   <2>4. QED
   502→\*     BY <2>1, <2>2, <2>3 DEF Inv
   503→
   504→\* <1>3. QED
   505→\*   BY <1>1, <1>2, PTL DEF Spec
   506→
   507→    
   508→\* THEOREM Consistent == Spec => []Consistency
   509→\* <1> USE DEF Ballots
   510→  
   511→\* <1>1. Inv => Consistency
   512→\*   <2> SUFFICES ASSUME Inv,  
   513→\*                       NEW v1 \in Values,  NEW v2 \in Values, 
   514→\*                       NEW b1 \in Ballots, NEW b2 \in Ballots,
   515→\*                       ChosenIn(v1, b1), ChosenIn(v2, b2),
   516→\*                       b1 =< b2
   517→\*                PROVE  v1 = v2
   518→\*     BY DEF Consistency, Chosen
   519→\*   <2>1. CASE b1 = b2
   520→\*     BY <2>1, VotedOnce, QuorumAssumption, SMTT(100) DEF ChosenIn, Inv
   521→\* (*
   522→\*     <3>1. PICK a1 \in Acceptors : VotedForIn(a1, v1, b1)
   523→\*       BY QuorumAssumption DEF ChosenIn
   524→\*     <3>2. PICK a2 \in Acceptors : VotedForIn(a2, v2, b2)
   525→\*       BY QuorumAssumption DEF ChosenIn
   526→\*     <3>. QED
   527→\*       BY <3>1, <3>2, <2>1, VotedOnce DEF Inv
   528→\* *)
   529→\*   <2>2. CASE b1 < b2
   530→\*     <3>1. SafeAt(v2, b2)
   531→\*       BY VotedInv, QuorumNonEmpty, QuorumAssumption DEF ChosenIn, Inv
   532→\*     <3>2. PICK Q2 \in Quorums : 
   533→\*                   \A a \in Q2 : VotedForIn(a, v2, b1) \/ WontVoteIn(a, b1)
   534→\*       BY <3>1, <2>2 DEF SafeAt
   535→\*     <3>3. PICK Q1 \in Quorums : \A a \in Q1 : VotedForIn(a, v1, b1)
   536→\*       BY DEF ChosenIn
   537→\*     <3>4. QED
   538→\*       BY <3>2, <3>3, QuorumAssumption, VotedOnce, Z3 DEF WontVoteIn, Inv
   539→\*   <2>3. QED
   540→\*     BY <2>1, <2>2
   541→
   542→\* <1>2. QED
   543→\*   BY Invariant, <1>1, PTL
   544→
   545→-----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   546→(*chosenBar == {v \in Values : Chosen(v)}
   547→
   548→C == INSTANCE Consensus WITH chosen <- chosenBar*)

   619→    [msgTset : TSet (Msg acceptor value ballot) MsgSet] [acSet : TSet acceptor AcceptorSet] (χ : State.Label → Type)
   620→    [χ_rep :
   621→      ∀ __veil_f,
   622→        Veil.FieldRepresentation (State.Label.toDomain acceptor value quorum ballot MsgSet AcceptorSet __veil_f)
   623→          (State.Label.toCodomain acceptor value quorum ballot MsgSet AcceptorSet __veil_f) (χ __veil_f)]
   624→    [χ_rep_lawful :
   625→      ∀ __veil_f,
   626→        Veil.LawfulFieldRepresentation (State.Label.toDomain acceptor value quorum ballot MsgSet AcceptorSet __veil_f)
   627→          (State.Label.toCodomain acceptor value quorum ballot MsgSet AcceptorSet __veil_f) (χ __veil_f)
   628→          (χ_rep __veil_f)]
   629→    [σ_sub : IsSubStateOf (@State χ) σ]
   630→    [ρ_sub : IsSubReaderOf (@Theory acceptor value quorum ballot MsgSet AcceptorSet) ρ] :
   631→    Veil.VeilM.meetsSpecificationIfSuccessfulAssuming
   632→      (@initializer.ext ρ σ acceptor acceptor_dec_eq acceptor_inhabited value value_dec_eq value_inhabited quorum
   633→        quorum_dec_eq quorum_inhabited ballot ballot_dec_eq ballot_inhabited tot MsgSet MsgSet_dec_eq MsgSet_inhabited
   634→        AcceptorSet AcceptorSet_dec_eq AcceptorSet_inhabited msgTset acSet χ χ_rep χ_rep_lawful σ_sub ρ_sub)
   635→      (@Assumptions ρ acceptor acceptor_dec_eq acceptor_inhabited value value_dec_eq value_inhabited quorum
   636→        quorum_dec_eq quorum_inhabited ballot ballot_dec_eq ballot_inhabited tot MsgSet MsgSet_dec_eq MsgSet_inhabited
   637→        AcceptorSet AcceptorSet_dec_eq AcceptorSet_inhabited msgTset acSet ρ_sub)
   638→      (@Invariants ρ σ acceptor acceptor_dec_eq acceptor_inhabited value value_dec_eq value_inhabited quorum
   639→        quorum_dec_eq quorum_inhabited ballot ballot_dec_eq ballot_inhabited tot MsgSet MsgSet_dec_eq MsgSet_inhabited
   640→        AcceptorSet AcceptorSet_dec_eq AcceptorSet_inhabited msgTset acSet χ χ_rep χ_rep_lawful σ_sub ρ_sub)
   641→      (@TypeOK ρ σ acceptor acceptor_dec_eq acceptor_inhabited value value_dec_eq value_inhabited quorum quorum_dec_eq
   642→        quorum_inhabited ballot ballot_dec_eq ballot_inhabited tot MsgSet MsgSet_dec_eq MsgSet_inhabited AcceptorSet
   643→        AcceptorSet_dec_eq AcceptorSet_inhabited msgTset acSet χ χ_rep χ_rep_lawful σ_sub ρ_sub) :=
   644→  by
   645→  veil_human
   646→  -- TypeOK: ge (maxBal a) (maxVBal a), i.e., tot.le (maxVBal a) (maxBal a)
   647→  -- After init: maxBal a = zero, maxVBal a = zero
   648→  -- Note: the acceptor is already introduced by veil_human
   649→  exact TotalOrderWithZero.le_refl _

   772→theorem initializer_AccInv (ρ : Type) (σ : Type) (acceptor : Type) [acceptor_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} acceptor]

   775→    [quorum_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} quorum] (ballot : Type) [ballot_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} ballot]
   776→    [ballot_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} ballot] [tot : TotalOrderWithZero ballot] (MsgSet : Type)
   777→    [MsgSet_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} MsgSet] [MsgSet_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} MsgSet] (AcceptorSet : Type)
   778→    [AcceptorSet_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} AcceptorSet] [AcceptorSet_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} AcceptorSet]
   779→    [msgTset : TSet (Msg acceptor value ballot) MsgSet] [acSet : TSet acceptor AcceptorSet] (χ : State.Label → Type)

   785→      ∀ __veil_f,

   787→          (State.Label.toCodomain acceptor value quorum ballot MsgSet AcceptorSet __veil_f) (χ __veil_f)

   789→    [σ_sub : IsSubStateOf (@State χ) σ]

   791→    Veil.VeilM.meetsSpecificationIfSuccessfulAssuming
   792→      (@initializer.ext ρ σ acceptor acceptor_dec_eq acceptor_inhabited value value_dec_eq value_inhabited quorum
   793→        quorum_dec_eq quorum_inhabited ballot ballot_dec_eq ballot_inhabited tot MsgSet MsgSet_dec_eq MsgSet_inhabited
   794→        AcceptorSet AcceptorSet_dec_eq AcceptorSet_inhabited msgTset acSet χ χ_rep χ_rep_lawful σ_sub ρ_sub)

   797→        AcceptorSet AcceptorSet_dec_eq AcceptorSet_inhabited msgTset acSet ρ_sub)

   809→  exact ⟨TotalOrderWithZero.le_refl _, fun _ _ _ _ h => absurd h Bool.false_ne_true⟩
   810→
   811→theorem Phase1a_Consistency (ρ : Type) (σ : Type) (acceptor : Type) [acceptor_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} acceptor]
   812→    [acceptor_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} acceptor] (value : Type) [value_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} value]
   813→    [value_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} value] (quorum : Type) [quorum_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} quorum]
   814→    [quorum_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} quorum] (ballot : Type) [ballot_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} ballot]
   815→    [ballot_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} ballot] [tot : TotalOrderWithZero ballot] (MsgSet : Type)
   816→    [MsgSet_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} MsgSet] [MsgSet_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} MsgSet] (AcceptorSet : Type)
   817→    [AcceptorSet_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} AcceptorSet] [AcceptorSet_inhabited : Inhabited.{1} AcceptorSet]
   818→    [msgTset : TSet (Msg acceptor value ballot) MsgSet] [acSet : TSet acceptor AcceptorSet] (χ : State.Label → Type)
   819→    [χ_rep :
   820→      ∀ __veil_f,
   821→        Veil.FieldRepresentation (State.Label.toDomain acceptor value quorum ballot MsgSet AcceptorSet __veil_f)
   822→          (State.Label.toCodomain acceptor value quorum ballot MsgSet AcceptorSet __veil_f) (χ __veil_f)]
   823→    [χ_rep_lawful :
   824→      ∀ __veil_f,
   825→        Veil.LawfulFieldRepresentation (State.Label.toDomain acceptor value quorum ballot MsgSet AcceptorSet __veil_f)
   826→          (State.Label.toCodomain acceptor value quorum ballot MsgSet AcceptorSet __veil_f) (χ __veil_f)
   827→          (χ_rep __veil_f)]
   828→    [σ_sub : IsSubStateOf (@State χ) σ]
   829→    [ρ_sub : IsSubReaderOf (@Theory acceptor value quorum ballot MsgSet AcceptorSet) ρ] :
   830→    ∀ (b : ballot),
   831→      Veil.VeilM.meetsSpecificationIfSuccessfulAssuming
   832→        (@Phase1a.ext ρ σ acceptor acceptor_dec_eq acceptor_inhabited value value_dec_eq value_inhabited quorum
   833→          quorum_dec_eq quorum_inhabited ballot ballot_dec_eq ballot_inhabited tot MsgSet MsgSet_dec_eq MsgSet_inhabited
   834→          AcceptorSet AcceptorSet_dec_eq AcceptorSet_inhabited msgTset acSet χ χ_rep χ_rep_lawful σ_sub ρ_sub b)
   835→        (@Assumptions ρ acceptor acceptor_dec_eq acceptor_inhabited value value_dec_eq value_inhabited quorum
   836→          quorum_dec_eq quorum_inhabited ballot ballot_dec_eq ballot_inhabited tot MsgSet MsgSet_dec_eq MsgSet_inhabited
   837→          AcceptorSet AcceptorSet_dec_eq AcceptorSet_inhabited msgTset acSet ρ_sub)
   838→        (@Invariants ρ σ acceptor acceptor_dec_eq acceptor_inhabited value value_dec_eq value_inhabited quorum
   839→          quorum_dec_eq quorum_inhabited ballot ballot_dec_eq ballot_inhabited tot MsgSet MsgSet_dec_eq MsgSet_inhabited
   840→          AcceptorSet AcceptorSet_dec_eq AcceptorSet_inhabited msgTset acSet χ χ_rep χ_rep_lawful σ_sub ρ_sub)
   841→        (@Consistency ρ σ acceptor acceptor_dec_eq acceptor_inhabited value value_dec_eq value_inhabited quorum
   842→          quorum_dec_eq quorum_inhabited ballot ballot_dec_eq ballot_inhabited tot MsgSet MsgSet_dec_eq MsgSet_inhabited
   843→          AcceptorSet AcceptorSet_dec_eq AcceptorSet_inhabited msgTset acSet χ χ_rep χ_rep_lawful σ_sub ρ_sub) :=
   844→  by
   845→  veil_human
   846→  -- Goal: show Consistency is preserved when Phase1a inserts a Phase1a message
   847→  -- Key insight: Phase1a only adds a Phase1a message, but Consistency/Chosen/VotedForIn
   848→  -- only care about Phase2b messages. So any Phase2b message in the new set
   849→  -- was already in the old set.
   850→  intro _ _ v1 v2 b1 q1 hv1 b2 q2 hv2
   851→  -- Extract the Consistency invariant from hinv (it's the first component)
   852→  obtain ⟨hcons, _⟩ := hinv
   853→  -- Use the pre-state Consistency invariant
   854→  apply hcons v1 v2 b1 q1 _ b2 q2
   855→  -- Goal 1 (reversed): show ∀ a, member a q2 → VotedForIn a v2 b2 (in the old set)
   856→  · intro a ha
   857→    obtain ⟨m, hcontains, htype, hval, hbal, hacc⟩ := hv2 a ha
   858→    refine ⟨m, ?_, htype, hval, hbal, hacc⟩
   859→    have hne : m ≠ { msgType := MsgType.Phase1a, acc := default, val := default, bal := b, maxVBal := default } := by
   860→      intro heq
   861→      rw [heq] at htype
   862→      simp at htype
   863→    rw [TSet.contains_insert_other _ _ _ hne] at hcontains
   864→    exact hcontains
   865→  -- Goal 2 (reversed): show ∀ a, member a q1 → VotedForIn a v1 b1 (in the old set)
   866→  · intro a ha
   867→    obtain ⟨m, hcontains, htype, hval, hbal, hacc⟩ := hv1 a ha
   868→    refine ⟨m, ?_, htype, hval, hbal, hacc⟩
   869→    have hne : m ≠ { msgType := MsgType.Phase1a, acc := default, val := default, bal := b, maxVBal := default } := by
   870→      intro heq
   871→      rw [heq] at htype
   872→      simp at htype
   873→    rw [TSet.contains_insert_other _ _ _ hne] at hcontains
   874→    exact hcontains
   875→
   876→theorem Phase1a_TypeOK (ρ : Type) (σ : Type) (acceptor : Type) [acceptor_dec_eq : DecidableEq.{1} acceptor]

Thinking

Now I have a clearer picture of what needs to be proven. Let me look at the TLA proof more carefully to understand the proof structure. The key insights from the TLA file are:

  1. For Phase2a_MsgInv2a (proving SafeAt v b for the new Phase2a message):

  2. Two cases based on hvb_or_allzero:

    • Case 1: allzero - all messages in S have maxVBal = -1 (zero in Lean)
    • This means no acceptor in Q voted in any ballot less than b
    • Use the last conjunct of MsgInv1b
    • Case 2: vb - there exists c < b such that all messages have maxVBal ≤ c and some message has maxVBal = c and maxVal = v
    • Need to show SafeAt v b by considering each ballot d < b:
      • If d < c: Use VotedInv (the vote at c was safe, hence v was safe at c, hence safe at d)
      • If d = c: The acceptor who voted at c voted for v
      • If c < d < b: No acceptor in Q voted at d (by MsgInv1b)

  3. For Phase2b_Consistency (proving Consistency is preserved):

  4. Two cases: b1 = b2 or b1 < b2 (WLOG)
  5. If b1 = b2: Use VotedOnce (quorum intersection + MsgInv2a uniqueness)
  6. If b1 < b2:
    • v2 must be SafeAt(v2, b2) (from MsgInv2a)
    • SafeAt(v2, b2) at ballot b1 gives us a quorum Q2 where every acceptor either VotedForIn(a, v2, b1) or WontVoteIn(a, b1)
    • ChosenIn(v1, b1) provides a quorum Q1 where all acceptors voted for v1 at b1. By quorum intersection, some acceptor a appears in both Q1 and Q2, so a must have voted for v1 at b1 and either voted for v2 or won't vote at b1. Since a can't both vote for v1 and not vote at b1, it must have voted for v2 at b1, meaning v1 = v2.

### What `hinv` Contains (The Full Invariants Assumption)

From line 92 in `/Veil/Frontend/DSL/Module/Util/Assemble.lean`, `Invariants` is assembled as the conjunction of all invariant clauses. For Paxos, this is a 6-tuple:

### What `has` Contains (The Assumptions)


Where:
- **quorum_intersection**: `∀ (q1 q2 : quorum), ∃ (r : acceptor), member r q1 ∧ member r q2`
- **AcceptorsUNIV_complete**: `∀ (a : acceptor), a ∈ AcceptorsUNIV`

### The Exact Goal at Line 2128

At the `fail` on line 2128, you need to prove the first part of the MsgInv2a post-condition for the **new** Phase2a message (the case where `m = newMsg`):

```lean
SafeAt v b
```

Which expands to:
```lean
∀ c, lt c b → ∃ Q, ∀ a, member a Q → (VotedForIn a v c ∨ WontVoteIn a c)
```

This says: For every ballot `c` strictly less than `b`, there exists a quorum `Q` such that for every acceptor `a` in that quorum, either:
- The acceptor voted for value `v` in ballot `c` (`VotedForIn a v c`), OR
- The acceptor will never vote in ballot `c` (`WontVoteIn a c`)

### Key Details

1. The goal requires you to **prove** that the newly-added Phase2a message satisfies the `SafeAt` predicate at the point it's sent
2. You have the **post-state's hypothesis** `hinv` which only gives you the *old* messages' invariants (via line 2131-2132 in the else branch)
3. In the **new message case** (line 2128), you cannot directly use `hinv.MsgInv2a` because the new message hasn't been verified yet - that's what you're proving
4. The construction on lines 2212-2239 of the Phase2a action builds the selected quorum `Q` and the message set `S` specifically to ensure this property holds

## Proof Context Analysis for `Phase2b_Consistency`

Based on my thorough examination of `/Users/dranov/src/veil-dev/Examples/TLA/Paxos.lean`, here's the exact proof context at line 2400:


So `t` is a Phase2a message where `t.msgType = MsgType.Phase2a` and `t.bal >= maxBal a`.

### 2. What is `hfilter` (the filter hypothesis for t)?

### 3. What are `val1`, `val2`, `bal1`, `bal2`, `quorum1`, `quorum2`, `hvote1`, `hvote2`?

After the second `intro` at line 2399, these are the decomposed components of the Consistency safety property:

**The goal structure** unfolds `Consistency: ∀ v1 v2, Chosen v1 → Chosen v2 → v1 = v2` as:
- `val1`, `val2` : `value` (the two values being compared)

- `quorum1` : `quorum` (a quorum of acceptors that voted for val1 in bal1)
- `hvote1` : `∀ a, member a quorum1 → VotedForIn a val1 bal1`
  - This is: `∀ a, member a quorum1 → ∃ m, msgTset.contains m msgs ∧ m.msgType = MsgType.Phase2b ∧ m.val = val1 ∧ m.bal = bal1 ∧ m.acc = a`
- `bal2` : `ballot` (the ballot in which `val2` was chosen)
- `quorum2` : `quorum` (a quorum of acceptors that voted for val2 in bal2)
- `hvote2` : `∀ a, member a quorum2 → VotedForIn a val2 bal2`
  - This is: `∀ a, member a quorum2 → ∃ m, msgTset.contains m msgs ∧ m.msgType = MsgType.Phase2b ∧ m.val = val2 ∧ m.bal = bal2 ∧ m.acc = a`

### 4. What does `hinv` contain (the full Invariants assumption)?

The variable `hinv` is in scope from `veil_human` (it comes from the assumptions passed to `meetsSpecificationIfSuccessfulAssuming`). It has the type `Invariants ...` which is a structure containing:

```lean
structure Invariants consists of:
  hConsistency : ∀ v1 v2, Chosen v1 → Chosen v2 → v1 = v2
  hTypeOK : ∀ a, maxBal a >= maxVBal a  
  hMsgInv1b : ∀ m, msgTset.contains m msgs → m.msgType = MsgType.Phase1b → ...
  hMsgInv2a : ∀ m, msgTset.contains m msgs → m.msgType = MsgType.Phase2a → ...
  hMsgInv2b : ∀ m, msgTset.contains m msgs → m.msgType = MsgType.Phase2b → ...
  hAccInv : ∀ a, 
    maxVBal a <= maxBal a ∧
    (maxVBal a ≠ zero → VotedForIn a (maxVal a) (maxVBal a)) ∧
    (∀ c > maxVBal a, ∀ v, ¬VotedForIn a v c)
```

As seen at line 1924 in Phase2a_Consistency: `obtain ⟨hConsistency, _, _, _, _, _⟩ := hinv` (6 components).

### 5. What does `has` contain (the Assumptions)?

The `Assumptions` structure contains the assumptions declared in the spec:

```lean
assumption [quorum_intersection] 
  ∀ (q1 q2 : quorum), ∃ (r : acceptor), member r q1 ∧ member r q2

assumption [AcceptorsUNIV_complete] 
  ∀ (a : acceptor), a ∈ AcceptorsUNIV
```

**Key assumption**: `quorum_intersection` states that any two quorums have a non-empty intersection (at least one common acceptor).

### 6. What is the exact goal at line 2400?

The goal at line 2400 (where `fail` appears) is:

```lean
⊢ val1 = val2
```

This is after introducing all the hypotheses. The proof needs to show that if `val1` and `val2` are both chosen (in their respective ballots via their respective quorums), they must be equal. This is the **Consistency invariant** - at most one value can be chosen by the Paxos protocol.

The proof would typically proceed by:
1. Using `quorum_intersection` to find a common acceptor `r` in both `quorum1` and `quorum2`
2. Extracting the Phase2b votes for this acceptor from `hvote1` and `hvote2`
3. Using the `AccInv` invariants to show that this acceptor could not have voted for two different values in different ballots

## Proof Plan for Theorem 1: Phase2a_MsgInv2a

### Goal
After Phase2a inserts the new Phase2a message with ballot `b` and value `v`, prove `SafeAt v b`.

**SafeAt v b** expands to: `forall c, lt c b -> exists Q', forall a, member a Q' -> (VotedForIn a v c \/ WontVoteIn a c)`

### Proof Strategy

```lean
-- At line 2128, replace `fail` with:

-- Goal: SafeAt v b
-- Unfold SafeAt: forall c < b, exists Q', forall a in Q', VotedForIn a v c \/ WontVoteIn a c
intro c hc_lt_b
-- Case split on hvb_or_allzero
rcases hvb_or_allzero with hallzero | hvb
```

#### Case 1: `hallzero` (All messages in S have maxVBal = zero)

**Key insight**: If all 1b messages have maxVBal = zero (meaning -1 in TLA), then no acceptor in Q has voted at any ballot less than b. We need to show `WontVoteIn` for all acceptors in Q.

```lean
-- Case: hallzero - all messages in S have maxVBal = zero
· -- Use Q as our witness quorum
  use Q
  intro acc hacc
  -- Need: VotedForIn acc v c \/ WontVoteIn acc c
  -- We will prove WontVoteIn acc c
  right
  constructor
  -- Part 1: forall v', neg VotedForIn acc v' c
  · intro v' hvoted
    -- hvoted gives us a Phase2b message x with x.bal = c and x.acc = acc
    obtain ⟨x, hx_contains, hx_type, _, hx_bal, hx_acc⟩ := hvoted
    -- By hquorum_covered and has.2 (AcceptorsUNIV_complete), acc is in AcceptorsUNIV
    -- So there exists m in S with m.acc = acc
    have hacc_univ := has.2 acc
    -- Extract from hquorum_covered that member acc Q implies exists m in S with m.acc = acc
    -- hquorum_covered is AcceptorsUNIV.all (fun a => !member a Q || S.toList.any (m.acc = a))
    -- This gives us witness m1b : Msg with m1b in S and m1b.acc = acc
    ...
    -- m1b is a Phase1b message with m1b.bal = b and m1b.acc = acc
    -- By MsgInv1b third conjunct: forall c', lt m1b.maxVBal c' -> lt c' m1b.bal -> neg VotedForIn acc v' c'
    -- Since hallzero: m1b.maxVBal = zero
    -- We have lt zero c (since c is a valid ballot < b, and zero is minimum)
    -- We have lt c b = m1b.bal (from hc_lt_b)
    -- By MsgInv1b: neg VotedForIn acc v' c
    -- This contradicts hvoted
    ...
  -- Part 2: gt (maxBal acc) c
  · -- From MsgInv1b first conjunct: le m1b.bal (maxBal m1b.acc)
    -- m1b.bal = b, m1b.acc = acc, so le b (maxBal acc)
    -- From hc_lt_b: lt c b
    -- Therefore gt (maxBal acc) c
    ...
```

**Key lemmas needed**:
1. Extract witness from `hquorum_covered` + `has.2` (AcceptorsUNIV_complete)
2. `List.all_eq_true` to destruct `hallzero`
3. `TSet.toList_contains_iff` to connect S membership with list membership
4. Totality lemmas: `lt_of_lt_of_le`, transitivity of le

#### Case 2: `hvb` (There exists c' such that some message has maxVBal = c' and maxVal = v)

**Key insight**: There exists `c' : ballot` with:
- `lt c' b`
- All messages m in S have `le m.maxVBal c'`
- Some message ma in S has `ma.maxVBal = c'` and `ma.val = v`

We case split on the relationship between `c` and `c'`.

```lean
-- Case: hvb - exists c' < b with the max ballot condition
· -- Extract c' from hvb (validBallots.any)
  -- hvb : validBallots.any (fun c' => lt c' b /\ ...)
  rw [List.any_eq_true] at hvb
  obtain ⟨c', hc'_valid, hc'_props⟩ := hvb
  simp only [decide_eq_true_eq, Bool.and_eq_true] at hc'_props
  obtain ⟨hc'_lt_b, hall_le_c', hexists_at_c'⟩ := hc'_props
  -- Now case split on the comparison between c and c'
  rcases TotalOrderWithZero.le_total c c' with hle_c_c' | hle_c'_c
```

##### Subcase 2a: `c < c'` (i.e., `le c c' /\ c != c'`)

```lean
-- Subcase: c < c' (d \in 0..(c-1) in TLA proof)
· -- We use VotedInv: the acceptor who voted at c' voted safely at c'
  -- Therefore by SafeAt c' v, there's a quorum for all d < c' including c
  -- 
  -- From hexists_at_c': exists ma in S with ma.maxVBal = c' and ma.val = v
  -- ma is a Phase1b message, so by MsgInv1b second conjunct:
  -- ma.maxVBal != zero implies VotedForIn ma.acc ma.val ma.maxVBal
  -- So VotedForIn ma.acc v c'
  -- 
  -- By MsgInv2b: the Phase2b vote came from a Phase2a message at ballot c'
  -- By MsgInv2a: SafeAt v c'
  -- SafeAt v c' at ballot c gives us the quorum we need
  ...
```

##### Subcase 2b: `c = c'`

```lean
-- Subcase: c = c'
· -- The acceptor ma.acc voted for v at c = c'
  -- All acceptors in Q either voted for v at c or have WontVoteIn c
  -- Use Q as witness quorum
  use Q
  intro acc hacc
  -- Get the 1b message from acc in S
  -- If that message's maxVBal < c, then by MsgInv1b third conjunct, no vote at c => WontVoteIn
  -- If that message's maxVBal = c, then by MsgInv1b second conjunct, VotedForIn acc m.val c
  --   and since all votes at c are for the same value (MsgInv2a uniqueness), m.val = v
  ...
```

##### Subcase 2c: `c' < c` (i.e., `c' < c < b`)

```lean
-- Subcase: c' < c < b
· -- All acceptors in Q have 1b messages with maxVBal <= c' < c
  -- By MsgInv1b third conjunct: for any ballot d with maxVBal < d < bal, no votes exist
  -- Since maxVBal <= c' < c < b = bal, we have no votes at c
  -- All acceptors in Q have WontVoteIn c (no vote + maxBal > c)
  use Q
  intro acc hacc
  right  -- WontVoteIn
  constructor
  · -- neg VotedForIn acc v' c for any v'
    intro v' hvoted
    -- Get acc's 1b message ma from S
    -- ma.maxVBal <= c' < c < ma.bal = b
    -- By MsgInv1b third conjunct: neg VotedForIn ma.acc v' c
    -- Contradiction
    ...
  · -- gt (maxBal acc) c
    -- From 1b message: le b (maxBal acc) and lt c b
    ...
```

## Proof Plan for Theorem 2: Phase2b_Consistency

### Goal
After Phase2b inserts a new Phase2b message (responding to Phase2a message `t`), prove Consistency is preserved.

**Consistency**: `forall v1 v2, Chosen v1 -> Chosen v2 -> v1 = v2`

Where `Chosen v` means `exists b Q, forall a in Q, VotedForIn a v b`.

### Available Hypotheses at Line 2400

- `t`: The Phase2a message being responded to (`t.msgType = Phase2a`, `ge t.bal (maxBal a)`)
- `val1, val2 : value`
- `bal1, bal2 : ballot`
- `quorum1, quorum2 : quorum`
- `hvote1`: `forall a, member a quorum1 -> VotedForIn a val1 bal1` (in post-state)
- `hvote2`: `forall a, member a quorum2 -> VotedForIn a val2 bal2` (in post-state)
- `hinv`: Pre-state invariants
- `has`: `(quorum_intersection, AcceptorsUNIV_complete)`

### Proof Strategy

```lean
-- At line 2400, replace `fail` with:

-- First, we need to case split on the relationship between bal1 and bal2
rcases TotalOrderWithZero.le_total bal1 bal2 with hle1 | hle2
```

#### Case A: `le bal1 bal2`

```lean
-- WLOG bal1 <= bal2
· by_cases heq : bal1 = bal2
  -- Subcase: bal1 = bal2
  · -- Both chosen at same ballot
    -- By quorum_intersection, get r in both quorum1 and quorum2
    obtain ⟨r, hr1, hr2⟩ := has.1 quorum1 quorum2
    -- VotedForIn r val1 bal1 (from hvote1)
    -- VotedForIn r val2 bal2 = VotedForIn r val2 bal1 (from hvote2 + heq)
    -- Both votes at same ballot => by MsgInv2a uniqueness, same 2a message => val1 = val2
    ...
    
  -- Subcase: bal1 < bal2 (lt bal1 bal2)
  · have hlt : lt bal1 bal2 := ⟨hle1, heq⟩
    -- val2 was chosen at bal2, so there's a Phase2a message for val2 at bal2
    -- By MsgInv2a, SafeAt val2 bal2
    -- SafeAt val2 bal2 at ballot bal1 gives quorum Q2 where all have VotedForIn val2 bal1 or WontVoteIn bal1
    
    -- val1 was chosen at bal1, so quorum1 all have VotedForIn a val1 bal1
    
    -- By quorum_intersection, get r in both quorum1 and Q2
    -- r has VotedForIn r val1 bal1 (from quorum1)
    -- r has VotedForIn r val2 bal1 or WontVoteIn r bal1 (from Q2)
    
    -- If WontVoteIn r bal1, that means forall v, neg VotedForIn r v bal1
    -- This contradicts VotedForIn r val1 bal1
    
    -- So VotedForIn r val2 bal1
    -- Both r voted for val1 and val2 at bal1
    -- By MsgInv2a uniqueness (same ballot => same 2a message => same value)
    -- val1 = val2
    ...
```

#### Case B: `le bal2 bal1` (symmetric)

```lean
-- Symmetric case: bal2 <= bal1
· -- Same argument with roles swapped
  ...
```

### Detailed Proof for Case `bal1 < bal2`

```lean
-- Subcase: bal1 < bal2
· have hlt : lt bal1 bal2 := ⟨hle1, heq⟩
  
  -- Step 1: Get a VotedForIn witness from quorum2 to find the Phase2a message
  obtain ⟨r2, hr2_mem⟩ := ...  -- any acceptor in quorum2
  obtain ⟨m2b, hm2b_contains, hm2b_type, hm2b_val, hm2b_bal, hm2b_acc⟩ := hvote2 r2 hr2_mem
  
  -- Step 2: Lift m2b to pre-state (it might be the new message or an old one)
  -- If m2b is the newMsg, then t.bal = bal2 and t.val = val2
  -- If m2b is old, it was in pre-state
  
  -- Step 3: By MsgInv2b, there's a Phase2a message for val2 at bal2 in pre-state
  -- (The Phase2a message must be in pre-state because Phase2b doesn't add Phase2a messages)
  obtain ⟨_, _, _, _, hMsgInv2b, _⟩ := hinv
  -- Get the Phase2a message for val2 at bal2
  ...
  
  -- Step 4: By MsgInv2a, SafeAt val2 bal2
  obtain ⟨_, _, _, hMsgInv2a, _, _⟩ := hinv
  obtain ⟨hSafeAt_val2_bal2, hUnique2a⟩ := hMsgInv2a m2a ...
  
  -- Step 5: Apply SafeAt at ballot bal1 (since bal1 < bal2)
  obtain ⟨Q2, hQ2⟩ := hSafeAt_val2_bal2 bal1 hlt
  -- hQ2 : forall a in Q2, VotedForIn a val2 bal1 \/ WontVoteIn a bal1
  
  -- Step 6: By quorum_intersection, get r in both quorum1 and Q2
  obtain ⟨r, hr1, hrQ2⟩ := has.1 quorum1 Q2
  
  -- Step 7: r has VotedForIn r val1 bal1 (from hvote1)
  have hvoted1_r := hvote1 r hr1
  -- Lift to pre-state (the new message is Phase2b for a at t.bal, not necessarily r or bal1)
  ...
  
  -- Step 8: r has VotedForIn r val2 bal1 \/ WontVoteIn r bal1 (from hQ2)
  rcases hQ2 r hrQ2 with hvoted2_r | hwont_r
  
  -- Step 8a: If VotedForIn r val2 bal1
  · -- r voted for both val1 and val2 at bal1
    -- By MsgInv2a uniqueness: all votes at bal1 are for the same value
    -- There's a Phase2a at bal1, and it has a unique value
    -- So val1 = val2
    ...
    
  -- Step 8b: If WontVoteIn r bal1
  · -- WontVoteIn means forall v, neg VotedForIn r v bal1
    -- But we have VotedForIn r val1 bal1 from hvoted1_r
    -- Contradiction!
    obtain ⟨hno_vote, _⟩ := hwont_r
    exact hno_vote val1 hvoted1_r
```

### Key Sub-lemmas Needed

1. **Lifting VotedForIn from post-state to pre-state**:
```lean
-- The new Phase2b message is for acceptor a at ballot t.bal
-- For other acceptors or ballots, VotedForIn witnesses lift
lemma lift_votedforin_pre (acc : acceptor) (v : value) (b : ballot)
    (hvoted_post : VotedForIn_post acc v b)
    (hnot_new : acc ≠ a ∨ b ≠ t.bal) :
    VotedForIn_pre acc v b
```

2. **Getting Phase2a from VotedForIn via MsgInv2b**:
```lean
-- From VotedForIn a v b, get Phase2b message, then Phase2a by MsgInv2b
lemma votedforin_implies_phase2a (hvoted : VotedForIn a v b) :
    ∃ m2a, contains m2a msgs ∧ m2a.msgType = Phase2a ∧ m2a.bal = b ∧ m2a.val = v
```

3. **Uniqueness of votes at a ballot (from MsgInv2a)**:
```lean
-- All votes at ballot b are for the same value (from MsgInv2a uniqueness)
lemma votes_at_ballot_unique (hvoted1 : VotedForIn a1 v1 b) (hvoted2 : VotedForIn a2 v2 b) :
    v1 = v2
```

# Plan: Prove Phase2a_MsgInv2a and Phase2b_Consistency in Paxos.lean

## Overview

Two theorems in `/Users/dranov/src/veil-dev/Examples/TLA/Paxos.lean` have `fail` placeholders that need complete proofs:

1. **Phase2a_MsgInv2a** (line 2128): Prove `SafeAt v b` for the new Phase2a message
2. **Phase2b_Consistency** (line 2400): Prove that Consistency is preserved after Phase2b

## Theorem 1: Phase2a_MsgInv2a (line 2128)

### Goal
After Phase2a sends a new message with ballot `b` and value `v`, prove:
```
SafeAt v b = ∀ c, lt c b → ∃ Q', ∀ a, member a Q' → (VotedForIn a v c ∨ WontVoteIn a c)
```

### Key Hypotheses
- `hvb_or_allzero`: Either all 1b messages in S have `maxVBal = zero`, OR there exists `c' < b` such that all messages have `maxVBal ≤ c'` and some message has `maxVBal = c'` and `maxVal = v`
- `hquorum_covered`: Every acceptor in Q has a 1b message in S
- `has.2` (`AcceptorsUNIV_complete`): Every acceptor is in AcceptorsUNIV
- `hinv`: Contains MsgInv1b which gives: for 1b messages, no votes in gap `(maxVBal, bal)`

### Proof Strategy

```lean
intro c hc_lt_b
rcases hvb_or_allzero with hallzero | hvb
```

#### Case 1: `hallzero` (all maxVBal = zero)
- Use quorum `Q` as witness
- For each acceptor `acc` in Q:
  - Get their 1b message `m1b` from S via `hquorum_covered` and `AcceptorsUNIV_complete`
  - Since `m1b.maxVBal = zero`, by MsgInv1b third conjunct: no votes in `(zero, b)`
  - Since `lt c b` and `zero` is minimum, no votes at `c`
  - By MsgInv1b first conjunct: `le m1b.bal (maxBal m1b.acc)` gives `gt (maxBal acc) c`
  - Therefore `WontVoteIn acc c`

#### Case 2: `hvb` (exists c' with max ballot)
- Extract `c' : ballot` from `hvb` with properties:
  - `lt c' b`
  - All messages in S have `le m.maxVBal c'`
  - Some message `ma` has `ma.maxVBal = c'` and `ma.val = v`

- Case split: `TotalOrderWithZero.le_total c c'`

##### Subcase 2a: `c ≤ c'` and `c ≠ c'` (i.e., `c < c'`)
- The message `ma` with `ma.maxVBal = c'` implies `VotedForIn ma.acc v c'` by MsgInv1b
- That vote came from Phase2a at `c'`, which has `SafeAt v c'` by MsgInv2a
- Apply `SafeAt v c'` at ballot `c` (since `c < c'`) to get the witness quorum

##### Subcase 2b: `c = c'`
- Use quorum `Q` as witness
- For each acceptor in Q, get their 1b message from S
- If `m.maxVBal < c`: by MsgInv1b, no vote at c, and `maxBal > c` → WontVoteIn
- If `m.maxVBal = c`: by MsgInv1b second conjunct, `VotedForIn m.acc m.val c`
  - By MsgInv2a uniqueness: all votes at `c` are for same value
  - The message `ma` voted for `v` at `c`, so this vote is also for `v`

##### Subcase 2c: `c' < c` (i.e., `c > c'`)
- Use quorum `Q` as witness
- All messages in S have `maxVBal ≤ c' < c`
- By MsgInv1b third conjunct: no votes in gap `(maxVBal, bal)` which includes `c`
- All acceptors have `WontVoteIn c`

### Implementation Notes
- Need to extract 1b message for acceptor from `hquorum_covered`:
  ```lean
  have hacc_univ := has.2 acc  -- AcceptorsUNIV_complete
  -- Use hquorum_covered which is a List.all, destruct with List.all_eq_true
  ```
- Use `TSet.toList_contains_iff` to convert between contains and list membership
- May need to prove the case `lt zero c` for valid ballots - this should follow from `lt c b` and `b ≠ zero`

---

## Theorem 2: Phase2b_Consistency (line 2400)

### Goal
After Phase2b sends a new message, prove:
```
val1 = val2
```
given that both `val1` and `val2` are chosen (via quorums `quorum1` and `quorum2` at ballots `bal1` and `bal2`).

### Key Hypotheses
- `hvote1`: `∀ a, member a quorum1 → VotedForIn a val1 bal1` (post-state)
- `hvote2`: `∀ a, member a quorum2 → VotedForIn a val2 bal2` (post-state)
- `has.1` (`quorum_intersection`): `∀ q1 q2, ∃ r, member r q1 ∧ member r q2`
- `hinv`: Contains MsgInv2a (SafeAt and uniqueness) and MsgInv2b (2b implies 2a)

### Proof Strategy

```lean
-- Case split on ballot ordering
rcases TotalOrderWithZero.le_total bal1 bal2 with hle1 | hle2
```

#### Case A: `le bal1 bal2`
```lean
by_cases heq : bal1 = bal2
```

##### Subcase A1: `bal1 = bal2`
- By `quorum_intersection`, get `r` in both `quorum1` and `quorum2`
- `VotedForIn r val1 bal1` and `VotedForIn r val2 bal1` (using heq)
- Both votes at same ballot → same Phase2a message by MsgInv2a uniqueness → `val1 = val2`

##### Subcase A2: `bal1 < bal2` (lt bal1 bal2)
1. Get Phase2b witness from `hvote2`, lift to pre-state
2. By MsgInv2b, get Phase2a for `val2` at `bal2`
3. By MsgInv2a, `SafeAt val2 bal2`
4. Apply `SafeAt val2 bal2` at ballot `bal1` (since `bal1 < bal2`) to get quorum `Q2`
5. By `quorum_intersection`, get `r` in both `quorum1` and `Q2`
6. From `quorum1`: `VotedForIn r val1 bal1`
7. From `Q2`: `VotedForIn r val2 bal1 ∨ WontVoteIn r bal1`
8. If `WontVoteIn r bal1`: contradicts `VotedForIn r val1 bal1`
9. So `VotedForIn r val2 bal1`
10. Both votes at `bal1` → by MsgInv2a uniqueness → `val1 = val2`

#### Case B: `le bal2 bal1` (symmetric)
- Same argument with val1/val2 and quorum1/quorum2 swapped

### Implementation Notes

**Lifting VotedForIn from post-state to pre-state**:
The new Phase2b message is `{msgType := Phase2b, acc := a, val := t.val, bal := t.bal, ...}`.
For any VotedForIn witness `m` in post-state that is not this new message, use:
```lean
have hm_ne : m ≠ newMsg := by intro heq; rw [heq] at hm_type; simp at hm_type
rw [TSet.contains_insert_other _ _ _ hm_ne] at hm_contains
```

**Proving uniqueness of votes at a ballot**:
From MsgInv2a: `∀ ma mb, Phase2a ∧ same ballot → ma = mb`
So votes at the same ballot come from the same Phase2a message, hence same value.

---

## Theorem 2: Phase2b_Consistency (line 2400)

### Goal
After Phase2b sends a new message, prove:
```
val1 = val2
```
given that both `val1` and `val2` are chosen (via quorums `quorum1` and `quorum2` at ballots `bal1` and `bal2`).

### Key Hypotheses
- `hvote1`: `∀ a, member a quorum1 → VotedForIn a val1 bal1` (post-state)
- `hvote2`: `∀ a, member a quorum2 → VotedForIn a val2 bal2` (post-state)
- `has.1` (`quorum_intersection`): `∀ q1 q2, ∃ r, member r q1 ∧ member r q2`
- `hinv`: Contains MsgInv2a (SafeAt and uniqueness) and MsgInv2b (2b implies 2a)

### Proof Strategy

```lean
-- Case split on ballot ordering
rcases TotalOrderWithZero.le_total bal1 bal2 with hle1 | hle2
```

#### Case A: `le bal1 bal2`
```lean
by_cases heq : bal1 = bal2
```

##### Subcase A1: `bal1 = bal2`
- By `quorum_intersection`, get `r` in both `quorum1` and `quorum2`
- `VotedForIn r val1 bal1` and `VotedForIn r val2 bal1` (using heq)
- Both votes at same ballot → same Phase2a message by MsgInv2a uniqueness → `val1 = val2`

##### Subcase A2: `bal1 < bal2` (lt bal1 bal2)
1. Get Phase2b witness from `hvote2`, lift to pre-state
2. By MsgInv2b, get Phase2a for `val2` at `bal2`
3. By MsgInv2a, `SafeAt val2 bal2`
4. Apply `SafeAt val2 bal2` at ballot `bal1` (since `bal1 < bal2`) to get quorum `Q2`
5. By `quorum_intersection`, get `r` in both `quorum1` and `Q2`
6. From `quorum1`: `VotedForIn r val1 bal1`
7. From `Q2`: `VotedForIn r val2 bal1 ∨ WontVoteIn r bal1`
8. If `WontVoteIn r bal1`: contradicts `VotedForIn r val1 bal1`
9. So `VotedForIn r val2 bal1`
10. Both votes at `bal1` → by MsgInv2a uniqueness → `val1 = val2`

#### Case B: `le bal2 bal1` (symmetric)
- Same argument with val1/val2 and quorum1/quorum2 swapped

### Implementation Notes

**Lifting VotedForIn from post-state to pre-state**:
The new Phase2b message is `{msgType := Phase2b, acc := a, val := t.val, bal := t.bal, ...}`.
For any VotedForIn witness `m` in post-state that is not this new message, use:
```lean
have hm_ne : m ≠ newMsg := by intro heq; rw [heq] at hm_type; simp at hm_type
rw [TSet.contains_insert_other _ _ _ hm_ne] at hm_contains
```

**Proving uniqueness of votes at a ballot**:
From MsgInv2a: `∀ ma mb, Phase2a ∧ same ballot → ma = mb`
So votes at the same ballot come from the same Phase2a message, hence same value.

  2181→        exact hUnique ma hma_contains hma_type hma_bal
  2182→

                -- But wait, adding it changes the spec and requires proving preservation.

                -- - We want to use WontVoteIn a zero
                -- - WontVoteIn a zero = (∀ v', ¬VotedForIn a v' zero) ∧ gt (maxBal a) zero
                -- - The second part: gt (maxBal a) zero = maxBal a ≠ zero (since le zero (maxBal a))
                --   From MsgInv1b: le m1b.bal (maxBal m1b.acc) with m1b.bal = b ≠ zero.
                --   So le b (maxBal acc), hence maxBal acc ≥ b > zero, so maxBal acc ≠ zero. ✓

  2773→            -- From hle_bal_maxBal: le m1b.bal (maxBal m1b.acc) = le b (maxBal acc)
  2774→            -- From hc_le, hc_ne: lt c b = (le c b ∧ c ≠ b)
  2775→            -- By transitivity: le c (maxBal acc)
  2776→            -- For the ≠ part: if maxBal acc = c, then le b c (from le b (maxBal acc))
  2777→            --   Combined with le c b (from hc_le), we get b = c.
  2778→            --   But c ≠ b (from hc_ne), contradiction.
  2779→            rw [hm1b_bal, ← hm1b_acc] at hle_bal_maxBal

Looking at the proof goal: SafeAt v b which is ∀ c, lt c b → ∃ Q', ...

We have lt c b which is le c b ∧ c ≠ b.

In the hallzero case, all 1b messages in S have maxVBal = zero.

For MsgInv1b's gap: ∀ c, lt m.maxVBal c → lt c m.bal → ∀ v, ¬VotedForIn m.acc v c

With m.maxVBal = zero and m.bal = b: - lt zero c means le zero c ∧ c ≠ zero, i.e., c ≠ zero (since le zero c is always true)

  • lt c b means le c b ∧ c ≠ b

So for c ≠ zero, MsgInv1b gives us no vote at c. For c = zero, I need a different argument. Looking at the TLA+ proof (lines 409-419 in Paxos.tla), the TLA proof avoids this issue entirely because -1 isn't in the Ballots set—the quantifier \A c \in Ballots never includes it.

In our Lean encoding with a generic ballot type, the quantifier ∀ c, lt c b → ... does include zero if lt zero b holds. The cleanest approach is to establish that Phase2a messages always have non-zero ballots, derive the same for Phase2b messages, and conclude that VotedForIn at zero is always false. I'd need to add this invariant after line 384 and prove it's preserved by each action. Alternatively, I could try showing directly that WontVoteIn acc zero is provable when c = zero, which might sidestep the quantifier issue entirely.

For WontVoteIn acc zero, I need both ∀ v, ¬VotedForIn acc v zero and gt (maxBal acc) zero. The second part follows because gt (maxBal acc) zero requires maxBal acc ≠ zero. From the 1b message invariant, le b (maxBal acc) holds. If maxBal acc were zero, then le b zero combined with le zero b would force b = zero, contradicting our assumption. So maxBal acc ≠ zero is established. For the first part, I need to show no vote exists at ballot zero.

-- AccInv: acceptor invariants
invariant [AccInv] ∀ a,
  -- maxVBal[a] <= maxBal[a]
  tot.le (maxVBal a) (maxBal a) ∧
  -- (maxVBal[a] >= 0) => VotedForIn(a, maxVal[a], maxVBal[a])
  (maxVBal a ≠ zero → VotedForIn a (maxVal a) (maxVBal a)) ∧
  -- For all c > maxVBal[a], no vote exists
  (∀ c, gt c (maxVBal a) → ∀ v, ¬VotedForIn a v c)

-- Phase2a messages have non-zero ballot (follows from action precondition `require b ≠ zero`)
invariant [Phase2a_nonzero_bal] ∀ m, msgTset.contains m msgs → m.msgType = MsgType.Phase2a → m.bal ≠ zero
